        # Implementation Plan: Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform (v5)

## Overview

Goal unchanged: make AA a peer of reigh-app on the sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform — renderer delegation to `<TimelineComposition>`, a Python `SupabaseDataProvider`, a `banodoco`-pool worker, CLI collapse, renderer parity tests. **Sequence support is deferred to a later sprint** (Phase 5 removed) — `timeline-schema` does not yet export `SequenceDraft` / `validateSequenceDraft` / `applySequenceDraftToTimeline` / `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES`, so the worker only handles `banodoco_timeline_generate` clip-level tasks in this sprint.

v5 is a mechanical cleanup of v4: the v4 overview captured the user-supplied corrected facts, but Step 3, Step 6, Step 9, Phase 5, and the success criteria still carried v3 wording. v5 propagates the corrections through the entire plan body so the executor can follow it without internal contradictions.

Key v5 corrections (all settled facts from user notes, verified against the filesystem):

- **Package `file:` paths**: point at the package ROOTS where `package.json` actually lives — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops` and `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema`. The `/typescript` subpaths have no `package.json` and would fail to install.
- **Versioned RPC signature**: `update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id uuid, p_expected_version integer, p_config jsonb)` — three params, no `project_id`. Verified against `../reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260326100000_add_timeline_config_version.sql`.
- **Worker write auth**: AA mirrors the reference `banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py` exactly. The DB call uses a **service-role Supabase client** (env: `REIGH_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`). The user_jwt from the claim envelope is JWKS-verified by `worker_jwt.py` for **authorization** (confirming the user owns the timeline), but the actual RPC invocation is service-role. SD-009's "service-role power gated to the worker" intent survives via this exact mechanism. AA never uses service-role against the user-facing edge functions (`timeline-import` is not used at all in this sprint; reads via `reigh-data-fetch` use a PAT or user JWT).
- **Claim protocol**: AA POSTs to `/functions/v1/claim-next-task` with body `{worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}`. Reference: `banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130`. As of reigh-app commit `f8599a887` (sprint-8 branch, local), the `claim-next-task` edge function accepts this shape (extended via migration `20260504120000_extend_claim_next_task_for_pools.sql`). The plan does **not** mirror generic `task_utils.py` (which historically only takes `worker_id`/`run_type`).
- **`config_version` from `reigh-data-fetch` is REQUIRED**: `TIMELINES_SELECT` in `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` is verified missing `config_version`. Phase 0 Step 2 ships a small reigh-app PR adding it. This is a hard prerequisite for Phase 2; not optional.
- **Sequence support deferred**: Phase 5, `scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs`, `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py`, `UnsupportedSequenceError`, `tests/test_sequence_support.py`, and the sequence success criterion are all removed.
- **Status reporting**: `update-task-status` with Title Case statuses (`Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled`) and structured `result_data: dict` carrying `{config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id}`. The pre-existing local `task-status` GET edge function (commit `ee2e6f10c`) surfaces `result_data` to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`. AA does not call `task-status` and does not call `complete_task`.
- **DataProvider surface**: required `load_timeline`, `save_timeline`, `load_asset_registry`, `resolve_asset_url`. Optional `register_asset`, `upload_asset`, `load_checkpoints`, `save_checkpoint`, `load_waveform`, `load_asset_profile`. Explicitly NOT present: `save_waveform`, `save_profile`, `load_profile`.
- **JWKS module name**: `artagents/core/reigh/worker_jwt.py` — mirrors the reference `banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py` so future readers can map between AA and the reference image.

Repository shape that matters (additions in v5 marked):
- `package.json` (**new at repo root**, `file:` deps to package roots), `scripts/node/{ops_helper.mjs,export_fixtures.mjs}` (**new** — `sequence_helper.mjs` removed).
- `remotion/src/Root.tsx`, `remotion/package.json`, `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/{timeline-composition,timeline-schema}/**`.
- `artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,cli.py,project.py,run.py,source.py}`.
- `artagents/core/reigh/{__init__.py,env.py,errors.py,supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,data_provider.py,task_client.py,worker_jwt.py}` (**new**, no `sequences.py`).
- `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py` (**new**).
- `artagents/timeline.py`, `artagents/packs/builtin/{reigh_data,open_in_reigh}/run.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`.
- `tests/test_project_*.py`, `tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`, `tests/test_reigh_data.py`, `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py`, plus new `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_task_client.py`, `tests/test_worker_jwt.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`. (No `test_sequence_support.py`.)

## Phase 2: Supabase Client + DataProvider Surface

### Step 5: Extract shared env + auth helpers (`artagents/core/reigh/env.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/reigh/env.py`. Move env helpers from `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py:19-105`.
2. **Add** `resolve_supabase_url(...)`, `resolve_claim_url(...)`, `resolve_task_status_update_url(...)`, `resolve_service_role_key(...)` (env: `REIGH_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`), `resolve_jwks_url(...)`.
3. **Update** `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` to re-export from `artagents.core.reigh.env` so `tests/test_reigh_data.py` keeps passing.

### Step 6: Implement `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring the canonical TS surface exactly (`artagents/core/reigh/{supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,errors.py,data_provider.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/errors.py` with `TimelineVersionConflictError` and `TimelineNotFoundError`. (No `UnsupportedSequenceError` — Phase 5 is deferred.)
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/supabase_client.py` — thin POST/RPC wrapper. Auth selector parameterised: `auth=("user_jwt", token)`, `auth=("pat", token)`, or `auth=("service_role", key)`. `update_timeline_config_versioned` is called as a Supabase RPC; a version-mismatch result maps to `TimelineVersionConflictError`.
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/timeline_io.py` with the mutator-based save:

   ```python
   def save_timeline(
       *,
       timeline_id: str,
       mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig],
       service_role_key: str,
       retries: int = 3,
       force: bool = False,
   ) -> SaveResult: ...
   ```

   Loop: load `(timeline, config_version)` via `reigh-data-fetch`, apply `mutator`, then call the RPC with the **exact three-arg signature**:

   ```python
   supabase_client.rpc(
       "update_timeline_config_versioned",
       params={
           "p_timeline_id": timeline_id,
           "p_expected_version": config_version,
           "p_config": new_config,
       },
       auth=("service_role", service_role_key),
   )
   ```

   No `project_id` is passed — the RPC takes only `(uuid, integer, jsonb)`. On version-mismatch result, re-fetch + re-apply the mutator, up to `retries`. Reject `expected_version=None` on the normal path; `force=True` is the only bypass and it logs.
4. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/data_provider.py` with `class SupabaseDataProvider` whose method surface matches `data/DataProvider.ts` exactly:
   - **Required**: `load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> tuple[TimelineConfig, int]` (returns `(config, config_version)`), `save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3, force=False)` (wraps `timeline_io.save_timeline`; takes `service_role_key`, NOT a user_jwt — the RPC is called with the service-role client per `worker_writes.py`), `load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id) -> AssetRegistry`, `resolve_asset_url(asset_entry) -> str`.
   - **Optional**: `register_asset(project_id, timeline_id, asset_entry)`, `upload_asset(local_path_or_bytes, *, content_type, user_id, timeline_id, filename) -> AssetEntry` (uploads to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}`, then `register_asset`), `load_checkpoints(...)`, `save_checkpoint(...)`, `load_waveform(asset_id)`, `load_asset_profile(asset_id)`.
   - **Explicitly NOT** present: `save_waveform`, `save_profile`, `load_profile`.
5. **Round-trip** every fetched timeline through `artagents.timeline.validate_timeline` / `load_timeline` / `save_timeline` so allowlist parity stays intact.

### Step 7: Update `open_in_reigh` to push via `SupabaseDataProvider` by default (`artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the default file-copy path with `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator=lambda t, v: merged_timeline, service_role_key=...)`. Keep `--print-sql` and `--copy-files` as opt-in escape hatches.
2. **Update** `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` to mock the DataProvider on the default path.

## Phase 3: Banodoco Worker (Mirrors banodoco-worker reference image)

### Step 8: Implement task-protocol client and JWKS verifier (`artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py`, `artagents/core/reigh/worker_jwt.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py` wrapping the **exact** task endpoints used by `banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130`:
   - `claim_next_task(*, worker_id, service_role_key) -> ClaimResult | None` — POSTs to `/functions/v1/claim-next-task` with body `{worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}`. Returns the SD-034 envelope `{ task_id, run_id, project_id, params, user_jwt, ... }` or `None`. Mirrors `banodoco-worker/worker.py`, NOT the generic `task_utils.py` shape.
   - `update_task_status(task_id, *, status, result_data=None, error=None, service_role_key)` — POSTs to `/functions/v1/update-task-status` with `status ∈ {Queued, In Progress, Complete, Failed, Cancelled}` (Title Case only) and optional `result_data: dict` (persisted into `tasks.result_data`).
2. **Document** in the module docstring that AA never calls `complete_task` (file/storage shape) and never calls `task-status` (that's reigh-app's `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reader).
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/worker_jwt.py` — verifies a user_jwt against the JWKS published at `resolve_jwks_url()`. Mirrors the reference `banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py`. Used **for authorization only** — confirms the user owns the timeline; the DB write itself uses service-role.

### Step 9: Implement worker (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/worker/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`. Module docstring records the resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with citations to `banodoco-worker/worker.py` (claim shape), `banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py` (service-role RPC), `banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py` (JWKS authz), and `task-status/index.ts` (commit `ee2e6f10c`).
2. **Reception**: long-running poll loop calling `task_client.claim_next_task(worker_id=AA_WORKER_ID, service_role_key=...)`. AA defensively rejects any envelope whose `task_type` is not `banodoco_timeline_generate`.
3. **JWKS authorization**: before any timeline write, verify `task.user_jwt` via `worker_jwt.verify(task.user_jwt)`. On failure (signature, expiry, audience, or ownership claim mismatch) the worker calls `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error='invalid user_jwt')` and skips. JWT verification is for **authorization** (proving the user owns the timeline); the DB write uses service-role.
4. **Snapshot fast-path**: if `params.current_timeline` is present, dispatch using it directly (skip `load_timeline`). Otherwise call `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id)`.
5. **Dispatch by `intent`** to existing AA executors. Each executor returns a mutator callable.
6. **Carry-fields**: every emitted clip populates `source_uuid`, `generation`, `pool_id`, `clip_order` (SD-003).
7. **Local provenance**: write `runs/<run_id>.json` with `metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload))` (SD-008).
8. **Timeline write**: `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id=task.params.timeline_id, mutator=executor_mutator, [REDACTED], retries=3)`. Internally this calls the three-arg `update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config)` RPC via the service-role Supabase client. `expected_version=task.params.expected_version` is required; `force=True` is forbidden on this path.
9. **Status report on success**: `task_client.update_task_status(task_id, status='Complete', result_data={'config_version': result.new_version, 'correlation_id': params.correlation_id, 'timeline_id': params.timeline_id}, service_role_key=...)`. The local `task-status` GET reader surfaces these fields to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`.
10. **Status report on failure**: `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id})`. Title Case only.
11. **Wire** `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into `artagents/pipeline.py:main` and `_print_entrypoint_help`.

## Phase 4: CLI Collapse — Operate on reigh-app Project UUIDs

### Step 10: Delete the placement schema (`artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,project.py}`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Delete** `artagents/core/project/timeline.py` and `artagents/core/project/materialize.py`.
2. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/schema.py`: remove placement symbols. Keep project/source/run schema constants.
3. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/project.py`: stop writing `timeline.json` from `create_project`; delete `load_project_timeline`/`save_project_timeline`. `show_project` either fetches via `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline` (if `project.json#project_id` is set) or reports cache-only state.
4. **Keep** `source.py`, `run.py`, `paths.py`, `jsonio.py`. Add an opaque `project_id` field on `project.json`.

### Step 11: Rewrite the `projects` CLI surface using direct timeline-ops primitives (`artagents/core/project/cli.py`, `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `artagents/core/project/cli.py` so subcommands are: `create`, `show`, `source add`, `list <project_id>`, `edit <project_id>`. Drop placement verbs.
2. **Create** `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs` (Node ESM) that imports **direct primitives** from the local `@banodoco/timeline-ops` (file: package root): `import { addClip, moveClip, setTimelineTheme } from '@banodoco/timeline-ops';`. Reads `{timeline, version, op, args}` JSON from stdin, dispatches by `op` to the right primitive, applies it, and writes the resulting timeline to stdout. **No** `createTimelineCommandRunner` (it doesn't exist in the local package).
3. **Wire** edit verbs (`add-clip`, `move-clip`, `set-theme`) so they shell to `node scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs` and use the resulting timeline as `mutator`'s return value inside `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3)`. `expected_version` (read from `reigh-data-fetch`'s `config_version`) is required.
4. **Update** `artagents/core/executor/cli.py:_cmd_run` so `--project` accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via `SupabaseDataProvider`. Keep the slug path as cache-only/offline mode.

### Step 12: Rewrite project-CLI tests (`tests/test_project_cli.py`, `tests/test_project_materialize.py`, `tests/test_project_schema.py`, `tests/test_project_runs.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `tests/test_project_cli.py::test_projects_cli_*` against mocked `reigh-data-fetch` + `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC (force a version-mismatch and assert the mutator is re-applied; assert exhaustion at N=3; assert that omitting `expected_version` raises rather than force-saving; assert the RPC is called with exactly `p_timeline_id`/`p_expected_version`/`p_config` and a service-role auth header).
2. **Delete** `tests/test_project_materialize.py` (placement-only).
3. **Trim** `tests/test_project_schema.py` to project/source/run schema only.
4. **Extend** `tests/test_project_runs.py` to assert `metadata.baseline_snapshot` is a sha256 hex string at exactly that path.

## Phase 5: Renderer Parity & Coverage Tests

### Step 13: New unit tests (`tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_task_client.py`, `tests/test_worker_jwt.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`: mock the Supabase client. Assert (a) `load_timeline` returns `(config, config_version)` and round-trips via `artagents.timeline`, (b) `save_timeline` re-applies the mutator on RPC version-mismatch and exhausts at N=3, (c) `save_timeline` raises when `expected_version` is omitted unless `force=True`, (d) the RPC is called with **exactly** `{p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config}` (no `project_id` parameter) and the auth header is the **service-role** key, never a user_jwt, (e) `upload_asset` writes to `timeline-assets/${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then calls `register_asset`, (f) the DataProvider surface is exactly the canonical set (presence + absence tests, including absence of `save_waveform`/`save_profile`/`load_profile`).
2. **Add** `tests/test_task_client.py`: mock `urllib.request`. Assert `claim_next_task` POSTs to `/functions/v1/claim-next-task` with body containing `worker_id`, `run_type='banodoco-worker'`, `worker_pool='banodoco'`, `task_types=['banodoco_timeline_generate']`; `update_task_status` POSTs to `/functions/v1/update-task-status` with **Title Case** statuses and `result_data` as a structured dict; `complete_task` is **never** invoked from anywhere in AA; `task-status` is **never** called by AA.
3. **Add** `tests/test_worker_jwt.py`: assert valid JWTs verify against a fake JWKS; expired/wrong-audience/missing-ownership-claim JWTs raise. The verification is independent of the DB write.
4. **Add** `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`: feed the worker a fake claim response. Assert (a) JWKS verification gates the write — invalid jwt → `Failed`, (b) intent dispatch, (c) snapshot fast-path with and without `current_timeline`, (d) on success: `update_task_status(status='Complete', result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id})`, (e) on failure: `status='Failed'` with `error` and `result_data={correlation_id}`, (f) carry-fields on emitted clips, (g) `metadata.baseline_snapshot` location, (h) the RPC is invoked with the **service-role** key (not the task's user_jwt).

### Step 14: Renderer parity smoke (`tests/test_renderer_parity.py`, `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs` — Node ESM that reads fixture timelines from `tests/fixtures/sprint08/` (JSON files snapshotted once from `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/testing.ts` via a one-shot `tsx` invocation against the reigh-app source tree). The local `@banodoco/timeline-schema` package does not export those fixtures; the JSON snapshot is the integration point.
2. **Add** `tests/test_renderer_parity.py`: shells to `node scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, compares hash to `tests/fixtures/sprint08/golden/`. Skip with a documented reason if the helper or goldens are unavailable.

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 0** — contract resolution + REQUIRED `config_version` PR + root `package.json` with `file:` deps to package roots.
2. **Phase 1** — renderer swap.
3. **Phase 2** — DataProvider with the canonical method surface, mutator-based retry against the three-arg `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC via service-role client, asset upload.
4. **Phase 3** — task-protocol client + JWKS authorizer + worker (mirrors `banodoco-worker` reference image module-by-module).
5. **Phase 4** — CLI collapse + node ops helper using direct timeline-ops primitives.
6. **Phase 5** — parity + new unit tests. (No sequence-support phase; deferred.)

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` and a manual headless render of `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`.
2. After Phase 2: `pytest tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py tests/test_reigh_data.py tests/test_open_in_reigh.py tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`.
3. After Phase 3: `pytest tests/test_task_client.py tests/test_worker_jwt.py tests/test_banodoco_worker.py` plus a manual end-to-end `claim → JWKS authz → service-role RPC → update-task-status(Complete, result_data) → reigh-app pollBanodocoTaskStatus` flow against staging.
4. After Phase 4: `pytest tests/test_project_cli.py tests/test_project_runs.py tests/test_project_schema.py`.
5. Final: full `pytest` and `tests/test_renderer_parity.py` against the sprint-08 golden bank.

      "description": "Phase 2 Step 6 \u2014 Implement the Python DataProvider stack at artagents/core/reigh/{errors.py, supabase_client.py, timeline_io.py, data_provider.py}. errors.py: TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError. supabase_client.py: thin POST/RPC wrapper with parameterised auth selector `auth=(\"user_jwt\"|\"pat\"|\"service_role\", token)`; expose an `rpc(name, params, auth=...)` helper. timeline_io.py: `save_timeline(*, timeline_id, mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig], service_role_key, retries=3, force=False) -> SaveResult`. Loop: load (timeline, config_version) via reigh-data-fetch; apply mutator; call RPC `update_timeline_config_versioned` with EXACTLY `{p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config}` (three params, NO project_id) and `auth=(\"service_role\", service_role_key)`. On version-mismatch, re-fetch + re-apply, up to retries; raise TimelineVersionConflictError on exhaustion. Reject expected_version=None unless force=True (and log when force is used). data_provider.py: class SupabaseDataProvider with EXACT canonical surface \u2014 required: load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> (TimelineConfig, int), save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3, force=False), load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id), resolve_asset_url(asset_entry); optional: register_asset, upload_asset (writes to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then register_asset), load_checkpoints, save_checkpoint, load_waveform, load_asset_profile. Explicitly NOT present: save_waveform, save_profile, load_profile. Round-trip every fetched timeline through artagents.timeline.validate_timeline / load_timeline / save_timeline so byte-equivalent allowlist parity stays intact. Watch: FLAG-012/issue_hints flagged that broadly handing service_role_key to non-worker callers conflicts with SD-009; treat service_role_key as worker-only and design the DataProvider so the CLI (T11) can call save_timeline with a non-service-role auth path or a different write entrypoint when SD-009 demands it. If the DataProvider must take service_role_key universally, document the deviation explicitly in module docstring.",

On failure: status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id}. Status enums Title Case ONLY.       {
        "id": "FLAG-001",
        "concern": "Auth lifecycle: Plan sends worker writes to `timeline-import` with a service-role key, but the current edge function rejects service-role-only requests and requires a user JWT, so the worker path is not executable as written.",
        "resolution": "Re-grounded every mechanism against the actual reigh-app sources cited in the gate. Pivoted worker auth: terminal `timeline-import` write uses an orchestrator-issued short-lived user JWT (not service-role, which the edge function rejects with 401). Replaced `push_timeline(timeline, expected_version)` with a mutator-based `save_timeline(mutator, retries=3)` so 409 retries can re-fetch + re-apply against the latest config_version. Pinned the full canonical DataProvider method set (load_timeline / save_timeline / load_asset_registry / resolve_asset_url + optional register_asset / upload_asset / checkpoint / waveform / profile). Added a Phase 0 cross-repo supporting change to extend TIMELINES_SELECT in reigh-data-fetch to include config_version. Switched asset upload to timeline-assets/${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename} + register_asset, dropping render-outputs/generations and signed-URL framing. Replaced the made-up LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY/THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY references with the public validateSequenceDraft / applySequenceDraftToTimeline / TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES API from sequence.ts via a node helper. Replaced the \"port timeline-ops to Python\" option with a node helper backed by createTimelineCommandRunner. Reworked Phase 3 onto the orchestrator's claim/complete/status protocol; worker honours optional current_timeline snapshot in the envelope (tests cover both shapes). Required expected_version for normal writes with explicit force=True opt-in. Pinned baseline_snapshot storage at runs/<run_id>.json#metadata.baseline_snapshot. Reconciled all three shared packages (composition + schema + ops). Pointed parity-test fixture imports at src/tools/video-editor/testing.ts instead of index.ts."
      },

        "resolution": "`../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/data/AssetResolver.ts` defines `loadWaveform?(assetId)` and `loadAssetProfile?(assetId)`, with no save waveform/profile methods; v2 success criteria list `load_waveform/save_waveform` and `load_profile/save_profile` as part of the exact surface."

Reworked Phase 3 onto the orchestrator's claim/complete/status protocol; worker honours optional current_timeline snapshot in the envelope (tests cover both shapes). The worker will not receive direct calls from reigh-app, so the ArtAgents worker plan must conform to the orchestrator's claim/complete/status protocol rather than just exposing an arbitrary HTTP endpoint or local loop.",
        "resolution": "v4 absorbs the post-cycle-3 contract resolutions provided in user notes as settled facts. Major mechanical shifts: (1) Worker no longer extends claim-next-task \u2014 the existing reigh-worker-orchestrator banodoco handler already implements the pool, AA mirrors banodoco-worker reference image. (2) Timeline write path switched from timeline-import edge function to update_timeline_config_versioned Postgres RPC (the versioned write the orchestrator handler uses). (3) JWKS verification of user_jwt added before terminal writes (new artagents/core/reigh/jwks.py). (4) Status reporting uses update-task-status's existing result_data: Record<string, unknown> field (NOT a JSON envelope inside output_location). result_data carries {config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id} which the local task-status GET edge function (commit ee2e6f10c, sprint-8 branch) surfaces to pollBanodocoTaskStatus. AA never calls task-status. (5) Status enum corrected to Title Case (Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled), never lowercase. (6) Root package.json uses file: paths to /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema}/typescript (the local packages are private and unpublished). (7) ops_helper.mjs uses direct timeline-ops primitives (addClip/moveClip/setTimelineTheme) instead of createTimelineCommandRunner (which lives in reigh-app source, not in the local timeline-ops package). (8) Sequence support documents a fallback path when validateSequenceDraft/applySequenceDraftToTimeline aren't exported by the local schema package. (9) Phase 0 cross-repo PR scope reduced to optional config_version extension only. DataProvider surface (resolution #6) was already correct in v3 and is preserved."

        Requirements:
        - Judge against the success criteria, not plan elegance.
        - Trust executor evidence by default. Dig deeper only where the git diff, `execution_audit.json`, or vague notes make the claim ambiguous.
        - Each criterion has a `priority` (`must`, `should`, or `info`). Apply these rules:
          - `must` criteria are hard gates. A `must` criterion that fails means `needs_rework`.
          - `should` criteria are quality targets. If the spirit is met but the letter is not, mark `pass` with evidence explaining the gap. Only mark `fail` if the intent was clearly missed. A `should` failure alone does NOT require `needs_rework`.
          - `info` criteria are for human reference. Mark them `waived` with a note — do not evaluate them.
          - If a criterion has `requires` capabilities that are not satisfiable by container workers (e.g., `drive_browser`, `subjective_judgment`), mark it `deferred_human` — NOT `fail` or `waived`. Deferred-human criteria do NOT count toward `needs_rework`.
          - If a criterion (any priority) cannot be verified in this context (e.g., requires manual testing or runtime observation), mark it `waived` with an explanation.
        - Set `review_verdict` to `needs_rework` only when at least one `must` criterion fails or actual implementation work is incomplete. Use `approved` when all `must` criteria pass, even if some `should` criteria are flagged.

        - baseline_test_failures in finalize.json lists tests that were already failing before execution. Do not flag these as rework items unless the executor introduced new failures in those same tests.
        - Review each task by cross-referencing the executor's per-task `files_changed` and `commands_run` against the git diff and any audit findings.
        - Review every sense check explicitly. Confirm concise executor acknowledgments when they are specific; dig deeper only when they are perfunctory or contradicted by the code.
        - Follow this JSON shape exactly:
        ```json
        {
          "review_verdict": "approved",
          "criteria": [
            {
              "name": "All existing tests pass",
              "priority": "must",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "Test suite ran green — 42 passed, 0 failed."
            },
            {
              "name": "File under ~300 lines",
              "priority": "should",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "File is 375 lines — above the target but reasonable given the component's responsibilities. Spirit met."
            },
            {
              "name": "Manual smoke tests pass",
              "priority": "info",
              "pass": "waived",
              "evidence": "Cannot be verified in automated review. Noted for manual QA."
            }
          ],
          "issues": [],
          "rework_items": [],
          "summary": "Approved. All must criteria pass. The should criterion on line count is close enough given the component scope.",
          "task_verdicts": [
            {
              "task_id": "T6",
              "reviewer_verdict": "Pass. Claimed handler changes and command evidence match the repo state.",
              "evidence_files": ["megaplan/handlers.py", "megaplan/evaluation.py"]
            }
          ],
          "sense_check_verdicts": [
            {
              "sense_check_id": "SC6",
              "verdict": "Confirmed. The execute blocker only fires when both evidence arrays are empty."
            }
          ]
        }
        ```
        - The parallel-review bookkeeping fields `checks`, `pre_check_flags`, `verified_flag_ids`, and `disputed_flag_ids` are optional for this criteria/task/sense-check review shape. Include them only when they are present in the output template or explicitly requested above.
        - `rework_items` must be an array of structured rework directives. When `review_verdict` is `needs_rework`, populate one entry per issue with:
          - `task_id`: which finalize task this issue relates to
          - `issue`: what is wrong
          - `expected`: what correct behavior looks like
          - `actual`: what was observed
          - `evidence_file` (optional): file path supporting the finding
          - `flag_id`: critique/review flag ID when applicable, otherwise `null`
          - `source`: short machine-readable source tag when applicable, otherwise `null`
        - `issues` must still be populated as a flat one-line-per-item summary derived from `rework_items` (for backward compatibility). When approved, both `issues` and `rework_items` should be empty arrays.
        - When the work needs another execute pass, keep the same shape and change only `review_verdict` to `needs_rework`; make `issues`, `rework_items`, `summary`, and task verdicts specific enough for the executor to act on directly.
